The previous shape budget maybe too short / low-degree. We can improve by:

Local full p optimizer:
Let's design simpler robust than previous:
```
def local_refine(p0, rng, time_limit, label):
    p = project_simplex(p0)
    n = len(p)
    best_p=p; best_score=exact_score(p)
    cur_score=best_score
    end = time + limit
    temp_factor = 0.25
    K = 16
    it=0
    since=0
    while time<end:
       c=conv(p); cmax=max(c); score=2n cmax
       # choose weights:
       # Phase 1: topK=32 when score high, topK=4 when close.
       # If score > target+0.01, T=0.2*cmax; else T=0.03*cmax
       if score > 1.55: T=0.15*cmax; K=32
       elif score > 1.52: T=0.05*cmax; K=8
       else: T=0.01*cmax; K=2
       w = topK_weights(c,K) or softmax T
       h = correlation(p,w); grad_p = 2*h  for surrogate max? If weights sum 1, gradient of weighted avg objective =2h. For descent use lr.
       # Projected gradient line search:
       gnorm = max(grad_p)
       step0 = 0.5 / max(gnorm, 1e-12) *? Need units. p scale ~1/n. grad_p ~w*p ~1/n? step ~n? Actually proj v=p-lr grad. If grad ~1/n, to change p by ~1/n, lr ~1. Use lr=1.0 or 0.1.
       Try lr in [1,0.5,0.2,0.05,0.01] maybe times base.
       For each: v=p-lr*grad_p; prev? momentum maybe.
       pt=project_simplex(v); sc=exact_score(pt)
       accept if sc < cur_score or sc < best_score.
       if no accept: random perturb in p or q; reduce temp.
```

### FFT correlation helper
```
def correlation_p_w(p, w, L):
    # p length n, w length >=1, need first n values h_j=sum_i p_i w_{i+j}
    L2 = max(L, p.size + w.size -1)
    Fp = fft(p, L2)
    Fw = fft(w, L2)
    h = ifft(Fp * np.conj(Fw)).real
    return h[:p.size]
```
But if L2 too small, wrap: need L2 >= p.size + w.size -1? For cyclic correlation no wrap, yes L >= n + (2n-1) -1 =3n-2. We can use grad_len = next_fast_len(3*n-2). Good.

### Convolution
```
def conv_sq(p):
    n=p.size
    if n==1: return np.array([p[0]*p[0]])
    L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    F=fft(p,L)
    c=np.real(ifft(F*F))
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c[np.abs(c)<1e-15]=0
    return c
```
For p normalized, sum c=1 within round.

### Score
```
def score_from_p(p):
    p = safe_normalize(p)
    n=p.size
    if sum <1e-12 return inf
    c=conv_sq(p); cmax=max(c); return 2*n*cmax
```
Potential lower bound? Use if cmax negative? no.

### Resampling
```
def resample_sequence(p, n):
    m=p.size
    if m==n: return p
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    q=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    return safe_normalize(np.maximum(q,0))
```
If p has zeros at edges, interpolation okay. Maybe use total count? For nonnegative step, linear area preserves. Good.

### Q setup
```
def setup_q(n, degree=16):
   global G_N,G_X,G_Z,G_B, G_BOUNDS
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   z=2*x-1
   cols=[]
   T_nm=np.ones(n); T_n=z.copy()
   for d in range(1, degree+1): # T_1 already
      if d==1: cols.append(T_n)
      else: T_np=2*z*T_n-T_nm; cols.append(T_np); T_nm,T_n=T_n,T_np
   # endpoint logs
   eps=1e-6
   cols.append(np.log(x+eps))
   cols.append(np.log(1-x+eps))
   B=np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64)
   # Maybe standardize? B values can be up to 1, logs large. For optimization, scale columns by their std to make parameters comparable.
   scales = np.std(B, axis=0)
   scales[scales<1e-12]=1
   B_scaled = B * scales  # then θ ranges smaller? If multiply B by scale, θ represents unit change q. We can just store B_scaled and bounds [-4,4] for unit q.
   G_B = B_scaled
   bounds = [(-4,4)] * len(cols) maybe endpoints maybe (-5,5)
```
Scaling B to unit std makes θ correspond to q rms. Bounds [-4,4] allow q variations up to 4 rms. Exponential ratio e^8. Good. Endpoint log columns have large values at endpoints (log eps=-13.8) small std? scaling handles.
But if B columns are orthogonal? Chebyshev not exactly on uniform grid but okay.

Constant shift: We exclude T0; but B columns may have nonzero mean. q can vary mean as function of θ; exp normalization removes mean. This means adding a constant to q with a combination of columns? No. But many directions may change mean only; objective invariant to mean, causing flat directions. Because no constraint sum p? Normalization makes p invariant to q constant, so if B has a component that is approximately constant, objective has flat. Chebyshev T0 absent; T1 etc have zero mean? Chebyshev T_m on uniform grid roughly zero mean for m>0. Endpoint logs not zero mean. They change shape. No pure constant except maybe if θ allowed to create? Not exactly. Flat directions minor. Could center each column: col = col - col.mean() to reduce mean changes. Let's do centering. Endpoint logs centered. This removes constant directions. Good.
`cols.append(col - col.mean())`.

Set bounds maybe [-5,5]. For DE, bounds list. Use same for L-BFGS.

### Q functions with globals
```
def q_make_p(theta):
   if G_B is None: return uniform
   q = G_B.dot(theta)
   # optionally recenter? Not needed; exp(max) stable.
   q -= np.max(q)
   p = np.exp(q)
   s = p.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: uniform
   return p/s
```
Since columns centered, θ=0 uniform.

`q_optimal_value(theta, exact=False, t_factor=None)`:
```
p=q_make_p(theta); return exact score if exact else surrogate
```
For DE, exact. For BFGS, surrogate.

```
def local_refine(p0, rng, time_limit, label):
    p = safe_normalize(p0)
    n=p.size
    if n<2: return p, score
    best_p=p; best_score=score_from_p(p)

    momentum=0.0

    it=0

      it +=1
      # adapt based on score

          topK = min(n, 64)
      elif best_score > 1.52:

          topK = min(n, 16)
      elif best_score > 1.50:

      gmax = max(g)
      if gmax <=1e-14: break? random.
```

We need weighted objective value to line search: if weights fixed, F=∑ w_k c_k. It can be computed as `sum(w*c)`; gradient=2h. Descent. Use surrogate `F_sur = float(np.sum(w*c))`. for lr in lrs:

### `safe_normalize`
```
def safe_normalize(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum()
    if p.size==0 or s<=1e-300:
       return np.ones(max(1,p.size))/max(1,p.size)
    return p/s
```

### `conv_sq`
```
def conv_sq(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    n=p.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    if n==1: return np.array([float(p[0]*p[0])])
    L=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    F=fft(p,L)
    c=np.real(ifft(F*F))
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c=np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    # numerical negatives
    if c.size:
       c[c<0]=0.0? But tiny negative; large negative shouldn't. Use c=np.maximum(c,0)
    return c
```
For p normalized, sum c≈1 but if clip negatives changes. OK.

```
def score_from_p(p):

    c=conv_sq(p)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    if not finite or cmax<=0: return 2.0? Actually if p uniform? if cmax=0 impossible. If cmax very small, score 0 due underflow? return 2*n*cmax.
    return float(2.0*n*cmax)
```

### Weights
```
def softmax_weights(c, T):
   c=np.asarray; if c.size==0 return empty
   cmax=max(c,0)
   T=max(T,1e-300)
   diff=(c-cmax)/T
   e=np.exp(np.minimum(0.0,diff)) # since diff<=0 stable
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1
   else w=e/s
   return w
```
Careful: diff can be positive due cmax? max c; no. Use `np.exp(diff)` with diff<=0.

`topk_weights(c,K)`:
```
def topk_weights(c,K):
   n=c.size
   if n==0: return empty
   K=int(max(1,min(n,K)))
   idx=np.argpartition(c, n-K)[-K:] if K<n else np.arange(n)
   # To be uniform over topK, but if values close? topK all equal weight.
   w=np.zeros(n); w[idx]=1.0/K
   return w
```
If rounded, okay. For near ties, any topK.

### correlation_p_w
```
def correlation_p_w(p,w,L):
   n=p.size
   m=w.size
   if L < n + m -1: L=next_fast_len(n+m-1)
   Fp=fft(p,L); Fw=fft(w,L)
   h=np.real(ifft(Fp * np.conj(Fw)))
   h=np.nan_to_num(h)
   return h[:n]
```
We can precompute `GRAD_LEN = next_fast_len(3*n-2)` globally for local? But for q, n fixed. Use helper `grad_len(n)` top-level.

```
def score_len(n): return next_fast_len(max(1,2*n-1))
def grad_len(n): return next_fast_len(max(1,3*n-2))
```

### simplex projection
Use previous code. Need ensure if v sum? Projection onto sum=1. If v all negative, uniform.
```
def project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float)
    n=v.size
    if n==0: return v
    v=np.where(np.isfinite(v),v,0)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssum=np.cumsum(u)
    idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
    cond=(u*idx) > (cssum-1.0)
    if not any cond: return ones/n
    k=int(np.count_nonzero(cond)-1)
    theta=(cssum[k]-1.0)/(k+1)
    out=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
    s=out.sum()
    if s<=1e-300: return ones/n
    return out/s
```
This projects onto sum 1. Good.

### q setup
```
def setup_q_space(n, degree=16):
   global QN,QB,QBOUNDS,QX
   n=max(4,int(n))
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   z=2*x-1
   cols=[]
   # center each col
   T_nm=np.ones(n)
   T_n=z.copy()
   for d in range(1, degree+1):
       if d==1:
          col=T_n.copy()
       else:
          T_np=2*z*T_n - T_nm
          T_nm=T_n; T_n=T_np
          col=T_np
       col=col - col.mean()
       cols.append(col)
   eps=1e-5
   logleft=np.log(x+eps); logleft-=logleft.mean(); cols.append(logleft)
   logright=np.log(1.0-x+eps); logright-=logright.mean(); cols.append(logright)
   B0=np.column_stack(cols)
   std=np.std(B0, axis=0)
   std=np.where(std>1e-8,std,1.0)
   B=B0*std
   # remove columns with zero norm
   # Bounds: general 5, endpoints 6
   bounds=[(-5.0,5.0)]*len(cols)
   # maybe endpoints larger
   if len(bounds)>=2:
      bounds[-2]=(-6.0,6.0); bounds[-1]=(-6.0,6.0)
   QN=n; QB=B; QBOUNDS=bounds
```
`B0*std` means if std large, θ small? Actually col scaled by std, so unit θ gives q rms=1. Good.

If Chebyshev columns not centered exactly after scaling. Good.

### q_make_p
```
def q_make_p(theta):
   global QB,QN
   if QB is None or QN<=0: return np.ones(QN)/QN
   theta=np.asarray(theta,float)
   if theta.size != QB.shape[1]: pad/trunc
   q=QB.dot(theta)
   if not all finite: return uniform
   q -= np.max(q)
   p=np.exp(q)
   s=p.sum()
   if not finite or s<=1e-300: return uniform
   p/=s
   p=p/ p.sum() # enforce
   return p
```
If theta too large, q range huge, exp of small underflow but max=0; some p zero. Fine.

### q surrogate exception safe
```
def q_surrogate_grad(theta, t_factor=None):
   if t_factor is None: t_factor=QTFACTOR
   p=q_make_p(theta)
   c=conv_sq(p); n=p.size
   cmax=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
   if cmax<=1e-300:
      return 0.0, np.zeros(QB.shape[1])
   T=max(float(t_factor)*cmax, 1e-12)
   diff=(c-cmax)/T
   e=np.exp(diff) # diff<=0
   s=e.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: w=zeros; w[argmax]=1; s=1
   else w=e/s
   h=correlation_p_w(p,w,grad_len(n))
   g=2*n*? Wait objective=2n*Surr, Sur= cmax+T log s. Gradient of Sur=2h. So grad_p=4n h.
   grad_p=4*n*h
   grad_p=np.nan_to_num(grad_p)
   v=p*grad_p
   # if B global not None
   g1=QB.T.dot(v)
   mu=QB.T.dot(p)
   sv=float(np.sum(v))
   grad_theta=g1 - sv*mu
   grad_theta=np.nan_to_num(grad_theta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
   surr=cmax+T*math.log(float(s))
   obj=2*n*surr
   return obj, grad_theta
```

### LP function
Top-level. Need no closures.
```
def lp_best_response(p, best_score, budget_s):
   if not HAVE_LP: return p,best_score
   n=p.size
   if n<4 or n>700: return p,best_score
   if budget_s <=2: return p,best_score
   c=conv_sq(p); M=float(np.max(c))
   if M<=0: return p,best_score
   p_norm=safe_normalize(p); fs=p_norm.sum(); p_norm/=fs
   # build sparse A
   rows=[] cols=[] data=[]
   # For each j, p_j contributes to rows k=j..j+n-1? c_k=∑ p_i p_{k-i}; for fixed g_j in p*g, term p_i*g_j when i+j? Let's derive p fixed, variable g. (p*g)_k = ∑_{i+j=k} p_i g_j. For fixed j, k=i+j with i=0..n-1 => k=j..j+n-1. Coefficient p_i. So row k, col j, data p_{k-j}. Build:
   for j in range(n):
       i_min=0; i_max=n-1
       for i in range(n): row=j+i; col=j; data=p[i]
   That's n^2 entries 490k for 700, okay but Python loop too slow. Use vectorized:
   rows = np.repeat(np.arange(n,n+2n-1)? no each j)
   i = np.arange(n)
   j = np.arange(n)
   rows = (j[:,None] + i[None,:]).ravel()
   cols = np.repeat(j, n)
   data = np.tile(p, n)
   A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(2n-1,n))
   res=linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(2n-1,M), bounds=(0,None), method='highs', options={'disp':False})
   g=res.x or None
   g=np.where(finite,max 0)
   s=sum; if s>0: g/=s
   for t in (1,0.8,0.6,0.35,0.15):
      q=project_simplex((1-t)*p_norm + t*g)
      sc=score_from_p(q)
      if sc<best: update
   return best_p,best_score
```
Build data np.tile(p,n) length n^2. Good. Memory for n=700 490k floats ~4MB. rows/cols int64 each ~4MB. Sparse matrix maybe ~15MB. OK.
Limit n<=600 to be safe? Previous 700. Use 700.
Time maybe 5-20s. Use only if budget>30.

```
def add_candidate(cands, p, sc, limit=8):
   if p is None: return
   p=np.asarray(p,float)
   if p.size==0: return
   p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0); p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum()
   if s<=1e-300: return
   p/=s
   if not np.isfinite(sc): sc=score_from_p(p)
   if not np.isfinite(sc): return
   cands.append((float(sc), p.copy()))
   if len(cands)>limit:

      del cands[limit-1:]
```

#### Imports and fallback

```
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft
    def next_fast_len(n):
```

#### Top-level helper: `now()` just `time.monotonic()`.

#### `resample_sequence`
```
def resample_sequence(p, n):
    p=safe_normalize(np.asarray(p,float))
    if p.size==0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    if p.size==n: return p
    old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,p.size)
    new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    q=np.interp(new, old, p)
    return safe_normalize(np.maximum(q,0.0))
```

#### Setup q: degree param
```
def setup_q_space(n, degree=18):
   global QN,QB,QBOUNDS,QLO,QHI
   n=max(8,int(n))
   x=...
   cols=[]
   T_nm=... T_n=z
   for d in range(1, degree+1): ...
   eps=1e-5
   l1=np.log(x+eps) - mean; l2=...
   B0=...
   # Check for tiny std, keep
   std=np.std(B0, axis=0)
   std=np.where(std>1e-8,std,1.0)
   QB=B0*std.astype(float)
   QN=n
   # bounds general
   nb=QB.shape[1]
   bounds=[(-5.0,5.0)]*nb
   if nb>=2:
      bounds[-2]=(-7.0,7.0); bounds[-1]=(-7.0,7.0)
   QBOUNDS=bounds
   QLO=np.array([b[0] for b in bounds])
   QHI=np.array([b[1] for b in bounds])
```
Could coefficient 18 plus endpoint 20. Good.

#### `q_make_p` stable
```
def q_make_p(theta):
    global QB,QN
    if QB is None or QN<=0:
       return np.ones(1)
    theta=np.asarray(theta,float)
    if theta.size != QB.shape[1]:
       tmp=np.zeros(QB.shape[1])
       m=min(tmp.size,theta.size)
       tmp[:m]=theta[:m]
       theta=tmp
    theta=clip_theta(theta) ? For make p, maybe clip to avoid insane after NM. But DE/loca within. Use clip.
    q=QB.dot(theta)
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
    qmax=float(np.max(q))
    if not finite: return uniform
    q=q-qmax
    p=np.exp(q)
    s=float(np.sum(p))
    if s <=1e-300 or not finite: return uniform
    p=p/s
    if not finite: uniform
    return p
```
If theta extremely large, nan_to_num posinf=100; exp okay. Good.

#### `clip_theta`
```
def clip_theta(theta):
    global QLO,QHI
    theta=np.asarray(theta,float)
    if QLO is not None and QLO.size==theta.size:
       theta=np.clip(theta,QLO,QHI)
    return np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
```

```
def q_exact_score(theta):

    p=q_make_p(clip_theta(theta))
    return score_from_p(p)
```

#### `q_objective_surrogate` and `q_jacobian`
```
def q_objective_surrogate(theta):
   val,_=q_surrogate_grad(theta)
   if not np.isfinite(val): return 1e100
   return val
def q_jacobian(theta):
   _,g=q_surrogate_grad(theta)
   return g
```

#### `polish_q(theta, budget_s, label)`
Top-level. It modifies global QTFACTOR. Returns tuple.
```
def polish_q(theta, budget_s, label=""):
    global QTFACTOR
    theta=clip_theta(theta)
    best_theta=theta.copy(); best_score=q_exact_score(theta)
    best_p=q_make_p(best_theta)
    end=time.monotonic()+max(0.0,float(budget_s))
    temps=(0.25,0.12,0.06,0.03,0.015,0.007,0.003,0.0015)
    if HAVE_SCIPY_OPT and QBOUNDS is not None:
       for t in temps:
          if time.monotonic() > end - 3.0: break
          QTFACTOR=t
          try:
             res=minimize(q_objective_surrogate, theta, jac=q_jacobian, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=QBOUNDS, options={'maxiter':120,'gtol':1e-8,'maxls':30})
             if res is not None:
                theta_res=np.asarray(res.x,float)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(theta_res)):
                   theta=clip_theta(theta_res)
                   sc=q_exact_score(theta)
                   if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<best_score:
                      best_score=sc; best_theta=theta.copy(); best_p=q_make_p(theta)
             # exact NM maybe after every t?
          except Exception:
             pass
       # Additional NM on exact from best
       if time.monotonic() < end - 5.0:
          try:
             res=minimize(q_objective_exact, best_theta, method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':300,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-8})
             ... update
          except: pass
    # Fallback random hill climb if no scipy? Could write simple coordinate.
    return best_theta, best_score, best_p
```
Nelder-Mead in 20 dims with exact may be many evals; maxiter 300 okay. But it may go out bounds; objective clips. Fine.

If no scipy, fallback: coordinate hill climbing with random steps on bounded theta:
```
bounds arrays; for iter while time: try random dimension step; if exact improves update.
```

### local_refine function code details
Let's write carefully.

```
def local_refine(p0, rng, time_limit, label=""):
    p = safe_normalize(np.asarray(p0,float))
    n = p.size
    if n < 4 or p.size==0 or time_limit <= 0:
        return p, score_from_p(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = score_from_p(best_p)
    cur_score = best_score
    prev_p = p.copy()
    end = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    L = grad_len(n)
    t_factor = 0.2
    topK = min(n, 32)
    momentum = 0.0
    it = 0
    since_best = 0
    since_accept = 0
    cons_surr = 0
    target = 1.5000
    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1
        old_p = p.copy()
        c = conv_sq(p)
        if c.size == 0: break
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            score = best_score + 1.0
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score; best_p = p.copy(); since_best=0; cons_surr=0
        cur_score = score
        # adapt weights
        if best_score > 1.60:
            t_factor = min(0.25, 0.25)
            topK = min(n, 64)
        elif best_score > 1.53:
            if t_factor > 0.08: t_factor = max(0.08, t_factor*0.95)
            topK = min(n, 24)
        elif best_score > 1.51:
            if t_factor > 0.03: t_factor = max(0.03, t_factor*0.95)
            topK = min(n, 12)
        else:
            if t_factor > 0.008: t_factor = max(0.008, t_factor*0.95)
            topK = min(n, 6)
        # topK at least 2
        topK = max(1, min(topK, n))
        if t_factor > 0.09:
            w = softmax_weights(c, max(1e-12, t_factor*cmax))
        else:
            w = topk_weights(c, topK)
        h = correlation_p_w(p, w, L)
        g = 2.0 * h
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        if np.all(g <= 1e-300):
            break
        F = float(np.dot(w, c))
        gmax = float(np.max(g))
        # line search lrs
        lrs = (0.02, 0.08, 0.25, 0.7, 1.5, 3.0, 6.0, 12.0)
        # adjust if gmax*n far from expected: if gmax*n > 10, divide lrs?
        scale = 1.0 / max(1.0, (gmax*n)) # if gradient larger, use smaller
        # If gmax*n ~1, scale=1; if gmax*n=10, scale=0.1.
        lrs = tuple(float(lr)*scale for lr in lrs)
        accepted = False
        tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(F))
        for lr in lrs:
            if not np.isfinite(lr) or lr <= 0.0:
                continue
            v = p - lr * g
            if momentum > 0.0:
                v = v + momentum * (p - prev_p)
            pt = project_simplex(v)
            c_new = conv_sq(pt)
            if c_new.size == 0:
                continue
            cmax_new = float(np.max(c_new))
            score_new = float(2.0 * n * cmax_new)
            F_new = float(np.dot(w, c_new))
            if not np.isfinite(score_new):
                continue
            if score_new < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = score_new; best_p = pt.copy(); p = pt; cur_score = score_new; prev_p = old_p
                accepted = True; since_best=0; since_accept=0; cons_surr=0
                break
            if score_new < cur_score - 1e-12:
                p = pt; cur_score = score_new; prev_p = old_p
                accepted = True; since_accept=0; since_best += 1; cons_surr=0
                break
            if F_new < F - tol and score_new < cur_score + 0.004:
                p = pt; cur_score = score_new; prev_p = old_p
                accepted = True; since_accept=0; since_best += 1; cons_surr += 1
                if cons_surr > 40:
                    # reset soon
                    p = best_p.copy(); cur_score=best_score; prev_p=best_p.copy(); cons_surr=0; accepted=True? break?
                break
        if accepted:
            momentum = min(0.35, momentum + 0.01)
        else:
            since_accept += 1
            t_factor = max(0.004, t_factor * 0.6)
            topK = max(1, topK // 2)
            # random perturb
            accepted = False
            sigmas = (0.1/n, 0.02/n, 0.005/n, 0.001/n)
            for sig in sigmas:
                pt = project_simplex(p + rng.normal(0.0, float(sig), n))
                sc = score_from_p(pt)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and (sc < cur_score - 1e-12 or sc < best_score):
                    if sc < best_score:
                       best_score=sc; best_p=pt.copy(); since_best=0
                    p=pt; cur_score=sc; prev_p=old_p; accepted=True; since_accept=0
                    break
            # pair move
            if not accepted and n > 1:
                hi = int(np.argmax(g)); lo = int(np.argmin(g))
                if hi != lo and g[hi] > g[lo] + 1e-300 and p[hi] > 0:
                    for frac in (0.2,0.07,0.02,0.005):
                        delta = frac * float(p[hi])
                        if delta <= 0: continue
                        pt = p.copy(); pt[hi]-=delta; pt[lo]+=delta; pt=project_simplex(pt)
                        sc=score_from_p(pt)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and (sc < cur_score - 1e-12 or sc < best_score):
                            if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_p=pt.copy(); since_best=0
                            p=pt; cur_score=sc; prev_p=old_p; accepted=True; since_accept=0
                            break
            if not accepted:
                since_best += 1
        if since_best > 800 or since_accept > 300:
            mix = 0.85 if best_score < 1.55 else 0.6
            p = project_simplex(mix * best_p + (1.0 - mix) * p + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05 / max(1,n), n))
            cur_score = score_from_p(p)
            prev_p = p.copy()
            since_best = 0; since_accept = 0; cons_surr = 0; momentum = 0.0; t_factor = 0.2
        if it % 3000 == 0:
            print(label + " it=%d best=%.10f cur=%.10f" % (it, best_score, cur_score), flush=True)
        if best_score < 1.4980 and since_best > 5000:
            break
    p = project_simplex(best_p)
    best_score = score_from_p(best_p)
    return best_p, best_score
```

### LP function building rows
```
def lp_best_response(p, best_score, budget_s):
    global? no
    if not HAVE_LP or p.size<4 or p.size>700: return p,best
    if time.monotonic()+1 > time+budget? no.
    p=safe_normalize(p)
    n=p.size
    c=conv_sq(p); M=float(np.max(c));
    if M<=0: return p,best
    Mvals=[M, 0.95*M, 0.90*M]
    time_end=time.monotonic()+budget_s
    j=np.arange(n)
    i=np.arange(n)
    rows=(j[:,None]+i[None,:]).ravel().astype(np.int64)
    cols=np.repeat(j, n).astype(np.int64)
    data=np.tile(p, n)
    obj=-np.ones(n)
    for Mv in Mvals:
       if time.monotonic() > time_end - 5: break
       try:
          A=csr_matrix((data,(rows,cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
          b=np.full(2*n-1, Mv)
          res=linprog(obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0.0,None), method='highs', options={'disp':False})
          if res is None: continue
          g=getattr(res,'x',None)
          if g is None: continue
          g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0); g=np.maximum(g,0)
          s=g.sum(); if s<=0: continue
          g=g/s
          for t in (1.0,0.8,0.55,0.3,0.1):
             q=project_simplex((1-t)*p + t*g)
             sc=score_from_p(q)
             if sc<best_score: best_score=sc; p=q.copy()
       except Exception:
          continue
    return p, best_score
```

### Potential flaw: internal_score using FFT linear convolution with `F*F` returns circular convolution. Need length L >= 2n-1. We use next_fast_len(2n-1). Good. But the result order? ### Potential flaw in gradient correlation: DFT convention of numpy: Ifft(fft(p)*conj(fft(w))) gives circular cross-correlation as derived. Need L>=n+2n-2. We use next_fast_len(max(L, n+m-1)) (m=2n-1). Good. But in local, `correlation_p_w(p,w,L)` with L precomputed grad_len(n)=next_fast_len(3n-2). If L < n+m-1? n+m-1=3n-2. Good. For q, same.

Let's verify with n=2 example and DFT formula using numpy convention: For real finite, x=[a,b,0,0], w=[w0,w1,w2,0]. FFT. ifft(X conj(W))_0 =? Derived with negative exponent in FFT and positive in IFFT: result_m=∑_i p_i w_{i+m mod L}. For m=1, p0w1 + p1w2 + p2w3? p2=0, w3=0. Good. For m=2, p0w2 (p1w3=0) but desired h2? We only need m=0,1. For n=2, h1 desired a w1 + b w2, correct. For n=3, L>=7. h_j for j=0..2: sum_i=0..2 p_i w_{i+j}; i+j max 4 (2n-2). If L>=7, no wrap. Good.

### One concern: Gradient of F=∑w_k c_k with weights for *convolution* c_k = ∑_{i+j=k} p_i p_j. For off-diagonal, the quadratic form matrix A_{i,j}=w_{i+j}. Since A symmetric? w_{i+j} symmetric in i,j yes. Derivative of p^T A p = 2 A p if A symmetric. But our quadratic form uses sum over all i,j? F =∑_k w_k ∑_{i+j=k} p_i p_j =∑_{i,j} w_{i+j} p_i p_j with i,j in 0..n-1. This includes each convolution term exactly once? def run_q_search(n, degree, rng, budget_s, init_p_list=None, label="q"):
```
    setup_q_space(n, degree)
    if init_p_list is None: init_p_list=[]
    start_thetas = make_q_starts(rng, init_p_list)
    end = time.monotonic()+max(0,budget_s)
    best_p=None; best_score=np.inf; theta_best=None
    scored_thetas=[]
    for th in start_thetas:
       th=clip_theta(th)
       sc=q_exact_score(th)
       if finite:
          p=q_make_p(th)
          scorered_thetas.append((sc,th))
          if sc<best_score: update
    scored_thetas.sort(key=qth_key)
    # DE
    if HAVE_SCIPY_OPT and end - time > 60 and dim <= 30:
       try:
          popsize=16 if dim>=20 else 14
          maxiter=80
          res=differential_evolution(q_objective_exact, QBOUNDS, maxiter=maxiter, popsize=popsize, tol=0.05, seed=int(rng.integers(0,2**31-1)), polish=False, init='latinhypercube')
          if res is not None:
             th=clip_theta(res.x); sc=q_exact_score(th); add
       except Exception:
          pass
    # polish top 4
    scored_thetas.sort(key=qth_key)
    top = scored_thetas[:4]
    for sc, th in top:
       rem = end - time
       if rem < 5: break
       limit = min(180.0, max(5.0, rem / max(1, len(top))))? If 4 starts, 45s each maybe too little. Better allocate 250 each but time budget 800. If budget 800, 4*200=800. Use limit = min(250, rem*0.6). For sequential, first gets more. Use `limit = min(220.0, max(5.0, rem * 0.45))`.
       th2, sc2, p2 = polish_q(th, limit, label)
       update
       rem = end - time
    # Add a few random local q polishes? if time remain
    if end - time > 60:
       for _ in range(3): th=rng.normal(0,1, dim); polish with limit=100
    return best_p,best_score
```

```python
"""
Search for low-peak self-convolution step functions.

The objective is
    score(p) = 2 n max_k (p * p)_k / (sum p)^2
for p >= 0.  The code searches in a smooth exponential/Chebyshev parameter
space, polishes with L-BFGS on a smoothed max surrogate, then refines directly
on the probability simplex using projected gradient / top-active-set moves,
random escapes, and optional LP best-response steps for shorter sequences.
"""

import time
import math
import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.fft import fft, ifft, next_fast_len
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft

    def next_fast_len(n):
        n = int(max(1, n))
        m = 1
        while m < n:
            m <<= 1
        return m

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_SCIPY_OPT = False

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    from scipy.sparse import csr_matrix
    HAVE_LP = True
except Exception:
    HAVE_LP = False

QN = 0
QB = None
QLO = None
QHI = None
QBOUNDS = None
QTFACTOR = 0.08

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027
LP_NMAX = 700


def cand_key(item):
    return item[0]


def qth_key(item):
    return item[0]


def score_len(n):
    return int(next_fast_len(max(1, 2 * int(n) - 1)))


def grad_len(n):
    return int(next_fast_len(max(1, 3 * int(n) - 2)))


def clip_theta(theta):
    global QLO, QHI
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64)
    if QLO is not None and QLO.size == theta.size:
        theta = np.clip(theta, QLO, QHI)
    return np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def safe_normalize(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(p.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(p.size)
    return p / s


def resample_sequence(p, n):
    p = safe_normalize(p)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == n:
        return p
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    q = np.interp(new, old, p)
    return safe_normalize(np.maximum(q, 0.0))


def conv_sq(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(p[0] * p[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    L = score_len(n)
    F = fft(p, L)
    c = np.real(ifft(F * F))
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c


def score_normalized(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = conv_sq(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax < 0.0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax)


def score_from_p(p):
    p = safe_normalize(p)
    return score_normalized(p)


def softmax_weights(c, T):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(c.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        w[0] = 1.0
        return w
    T = float(max(T, 1e-300))
    diff = (c - cmax) / T
    diff = np.minimum(diff, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(diff)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return e / s


def topk_weights(c, K):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(c.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    K = int(max(1, min(n, K)))
    w = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if K >= n:
        w[:] = 1.0 / n
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(c, n - K)[-K:]
        w[idx] = 1.0 / K
    return w


def correlation_p_w(p, w, L):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    m = int(w.size)
    if L < n + m - 1:
        L = int(next_fast_len(n + m - 1))
    Fp = fft(p, L)
    Fw = fft(w, L)
    h = np.real(ifft(Fp * np.conj(Fw)))
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return h[:n]


def project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssum = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cond = (u * idx) > (cssum - 1.0)
    if not np.any(cond):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(np.count_nonzero(cond) - 1)
    theta = (cssum[k] - 1.0) / float(k + 1)
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return out / s


def setup_q_space(n, degree=18):
    global QN, QB, QLO, QHI, QBOUNDS
    n = int(max(8, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    z = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    cols = []
    T_nm = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    T_n = z.copy()
    for d in range(1, int(degree) + 1):
        if d == 1:
            col = T_n.copy()
        else:
            T_np = 2.0 * z * T_n - T_nm
            T_nm = T_n
            T_n = T_np
            col = T_np
        col = col.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
        col = col - float(col.mean())
        cols.append(col)

    eps = 0.05
    l1 = np.log(x + eps).astype(np.float64)
    l1 = l1 - float(l1.mean())
    cols.append(l1)
    l2 = np.log(1.0 - x + eps).astype(np.float64)
    l2 = l2 - float(l2.mean())
    cols.append(l2)

    B = np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64)
    std = np.std(B, axis=0)
    std = np.where(std > 1e-8, std, 1.0)
    B = B * std.astype(np.float64)
    B = np.nan_to_num(B, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    nb = B.shape[1]
    bounds = [(-5.0, 5.0)] * nb
    if nb >= 2:
        bounds[-2] = (-7.0, 7.0)
        bounds[-1] = (-7.0, 7.0)

    QN = n
    QB = B
    QBOUNDS = bounds
    QLO = np.array([b[0] for b in bounds], dtype=np.float64)
    QHI = np.array([b[1] for b in bounds], dtype=np.float64)


def q_make_p(theta):
    global QB, QN
    if QB is None or QN <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta = clip_theta(theta)
    if theta.size != QB.shape[1]:
        t2 = np.zeros(QB.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
        m = min(t2.size, theta.size)
        t2[:m] = theta[:m]
        theta = t2
    q = QB.dot(theta)
    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
    qmax = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(qmax):
        return np.full(QN, 1.0 / QN, dtype=np.float64)
    q = q - qmax
    p = np.exp(q)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(QN, 1.0 / QN, dtype=np.float64)
    p = p / s
    p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s2 = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(QN, 1.0 / QN, dtype=np.float64)
    return p / s2


def q_exact_score(theta):
    global QB
    if QB is None:
        return np.inf
    p = q_make_p(theta)
    return score_normalized(p)


def q_objective_exact(theta):
    val = q_exact_score(theta)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100
    return float(val)


def q_surrogate_grad(theta, t_factor=None):
    global QB, QTFACTOR
    if QB is None:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if t_factor is None:
        t_factor = QTFACTOR
    p = q_make_p(theta)
    n = int(p.size)
    c = conv_sq(p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(QB.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 1e-300:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(QB.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    T = max(float(t_factor) * cmax, 1e-12)
    diff = (c - cmax) / T
    diff = np.minimum(diff, 0.0)
    e = np.exp(diff)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros_like(c, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        s = 1.0
    else:
        w = e / s

    h = correlation_p_w(p, w, grad_len(n))
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    # Objective used by L-BFGS is 2*n*(smoothed max), gradient is 4*n*h.
    grad_p = 4.0 * float(n) * h
    grad_p = np.nan_to_num(grad_p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    v = p * grad_p
    B = QB
    g1 = B.T.dot(v)
    mu = B.T.dot(p)
    sv = float(v.sum())
    grad_theta = g1 - sv * mu
    grad_theta = np.nan_to_num(grad_theta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

    surr = cmax + T * math.log(s)
    obj = 2.0 * float(n) * surr
    if not np.isfinite(obj):
        obj = 1e100
    return float(obj), grad_theta


def q_objective_surrogate(theta):
    val, _ = q_surrogate_grad(theta)
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return 1e100
    return val


def q_jacobian(theta):
    _, g = q_surrogate_grad(theta)
    return g


def fit_q_from_p(p):
    global QB, QN, QLO, QHI
    if QB is None:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    p = safe_normalize(resample_sequence(p, QN))
    logp = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-12))
    logp = logp - float(logp.mean())
    try:
        theta, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(QB, logp, rcond=None)
    except Exception:
        theta = np.zeros(QB.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
    theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return clip_theta(theta)


def make_q_starts(rng, init_p_list):
    global QB
    if QB is None:
        return []
    dim = int(QB.shape[1])
    starts = [np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)]

    for a in (-2.0, -1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
        th = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
        if dim > 0:
            th[0] = a
        starts.append(clip_theta(th))

    for a in (-2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0):
        th = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
        if dim > 1:
            th[1] = a
        starts.append(clip_theta(th))

    for a in (-1.0, -0.5, 0.5, 1.0):
        th = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
        if dim > 2:
            th[2] = a
        starts.append(clip_theta(th))

    for a in (-1.2, 1.2):
        for b in (-1.0, 1.0):
            th = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
            if dim > 0:
                th[0] = a
            if dim > 2:
                th[2] = b
            starts.append(clip_theta(th))

    if dim >= 2:
        for a, b in ((1.0, -1.5), (-1.0, 1.5), (1.5, -2.0), (-1.5, 2.0)):
            th = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
            th[-2] = a
            th[-1] = b
            starts.append(clip_theta(th))

    if init_p_list is not None:
        for p in init_p_list:
            if p is None:
                continue
            try:
                th = fit_q_from_p(p)
                if th.size == dim and np.all(np.isfinite(th)):
                    starts.append(clip_theta(th))
            except Exception:
                pass

    for _ in range(16):
        starts.append(clip_theta(rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, dim).astype(np.float64)))
    for _ in range(6):
        starts.append(clip_theta(rng.normal(0.0, 1.6, dim).astype(np.float64)))

    return starts


def polish_q(theta, budget_s, label=""):
    global QTFACTOR
    theta = clip_theta(theta)
    best_theta = theta.copy()
    best_score = q_exact_score(theta)
    best_p = q_make_p(best_theta)
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    if HAVE_SCIPY_OPT and QBOUNDS is not None:
        temps = (0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.007, 0.003, 0.0015)
        for t in temps:
            if time.monotonic() > end - 4.0:
                break
            QTFACTOR = float(t)
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    q_objective_surrogate,
                    theta,
                    jac=q_jacobian,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    bounds=QBOUNDS,
                    options={"maxiter": 140, "gtol": 1e-8, "maxls": 30}
                )
                if res is not None:
                    x = clip_theta(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
                    sc = q_exact_score(x)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = float(sc)
                        best_theta = x
                        best_p = q_make_p(x)
            except Exception:
                pass

        if time.monotonic() < end - 6.0:
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    q_objective_exact,
                    best_theta,
                    method="Nelder-Mead",
                    options={"maxiter": 350, "xatol": 1e-6, "fatol": 1e-9}
                )
                if res is not None:
                    x = clip_theta(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
                    sc = q_exact_score(x)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                        best_score = float(sc)
                        best_theta = x
                        best_p = q_make_p(x)
            except Exception:
                pass
    else:
        # Fallback coordinate hill climbing.
        dim = int(QB.shape[1]) if QB is not None else 0
        lo = QLO.copy() if QLO is not None else np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float64)
        hi = QHI.copy() if QHI is not None else np.ones(dim, dtype=np.float64)
        th = best_theta.copy()
        cur = float(best_score)
        while time.monotonic() < end:
            if dim <= 0:
                break
            j = int(rng.integers(0, dim))
            for sig in (0.5, 0.2, 0.05, 0.01):
                th2 = th.copy()
                th2[j] += float(rng.normal(0.0, sig))
                th2 = np.clip(th2, lo, hi)
                sc = q_exact_score(th2)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < cur:
                    th = th2
                    cur = sc
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_theta = th.copy()
                        best_p = q_make_p(th)
                    break

    return best_theta, float(best_score) if np.isfinite(best_score) else np.inf, best_p


def run_q_search(n, degree, rng, budget_s, init_p_list=None, label="q"):
    setup_q_space(n, degree)
    if init_p_list is None:
        init_p_list = []
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))

    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf
    best_theta = None

    starts = make_q_starts(rng, init_p_list)
    scored = []
    for th in starts:
        th = clip_theta(th)
        sc = q_exact_score(th)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            p = q_make_p(th)
            scored.append((float(sc), th.copy()))
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_theta = th.copy()

    scored.sort(key=qth_key)
    if HAVE_SCIPY_OPT and QBOUNDS is not None and (end - time.monotonic()) > 70.0:
        dim = int(QB.shape[1])
        if dim <= 30:
            try:
                popsize = 18 if dim <= 24 else 16
                maxiter = 80 if dim <= 24 else 55
                res = differential_evolution(
                    q_objective_exact,
                    QBOUNDS,
                    maxiter=maxiter,
                    popsize=popsize,
                    tol=0.05,
                    seed=int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)),
                    polish=False,
                    init="latinhypercube"
                )
                if res is not None:
                    th = clip_theta(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
                    sc = q_exact_score(th)
                    if np.isfinite(sc):
                        p = q_make_p(th)
                        scored.append((float(sc), th.copy()))
                        if sc < best_score:
                            best_score = float(sc)
                            best_p = p.copy()
                            best_theta = th.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    scored.sort(key=qth_key)
    top = scored[:5]
    for sc, th in top:
        rem = end - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 5.0:
            break
        limit = min(220.0, max(5.0, rem * 0.45))
        try:
            th2, sc2, p2 = polish_q(th, limit, label)
            if p2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc2)
                best_p = p2.copy()
                best_theta = th2.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    if (end - time.monotonic()) > 70.0 and QB is not None:
        dim = int(QB.shape[1])
        for _ in range(3):
            rem = end - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 10.0:
                break
            th = clip_theta(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, dim).astype(np.float64))
            try:
                th2, sc2, p2 = polish_q(th, min(120.0, rem), label + "-rand")
                if p2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc2)
                    best_p = p2.copy()
                    best_theta = th2.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = q_make_p(np.zeros(int(QB.shape[1]) if QB is not None else 1))
        best_score = score_normalized(best_p)
    return best_p, float(best_score) if np.isfinite(best_score) else np.inf, best_theta


def local_refine(p0, rng, time_limit, label=""):
    p = safe_normalize(np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64))
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 4 or time_limit <= 0.0:
        return p, score_normalized(p)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = score_normalized(best_p)
    cur_score = best_score
    prev_p = p.copy()
    end = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    L = grad_len(n)

    t_factor = 0.20
    topK = min(n, 32)
    momentum = 0.0
    it = 0
    since_best = 0
    since_accept = 0
    cons_surr = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end:
        it += 1
        old_p = p.copy()

        c = conv_sq(p)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        score = float(2.0 * n * cmax)
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            score = best_score + 1.0

        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_p = p.copy()
            since_best = 0
            cons_surr = 0
        cur_score = score

        if best_score > 1.60:
            if t_factor < 0.25:
                t_factor = max(0.20, t_factor * 1.05)
            topK = min(n, 64)
        elif best_score > 1.53:
            if t_factor > 0.08:
                t_factor = max(0.08, t_factor * 0.95)
            topK = min(n, 24)
        elif best_score > 1.51:
            if t_factor > 0.03:
                t_factor = max(0.03, t_factor * 0.95)
            topK = min(n, 10)
        else:
            if t_factor > 0.008:
                t_factor = max(0.008, t_factor * 0.95)
            topK = min(n, 3)

        topK = max(1, int(topK))
        if t_factor > 0.09:
            w = softmax_weights(c, max(1e-12, t_factor * cmax))
        else:
            w = topk_weights(c, topK)

        h = correlation_p_w(p, w, L)
        g = 2.0 * h
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        if float(np.max(g)) <= 1e-300:
            break

        F = float(np.dot(w, c))
        gmax = float(np.max(g))
        base = 1.0 / max(gmax * float(n), 1e-300)
        base = min(80.0, max(1e-3, base))
        rel = (0.05, 0.12, 0.28, 0.65, 1.4, 3.0, 6.0)
        lrs = [min(60.0, base * r) for r in rel]

        accepted = False
        tol = 1e-12 * max(1.0, abs(F))
        for lr in lrs:
            lr = float(lr)
            if not np.isfinite(lr) or lr <= 0.0:
                continue
            v = p - lr * g
            if momentum > 0.0:
                v = v + momentum * (p - prev_p)
            pt = project_simplex(v)
            c_new = conv_sq(pt)
            if c_new.size == 0:
                continue
            cmax_new = float(np.max(c_new))
            score_new = float(2.0 * n * cmax_new)
            F_new = float(np.dot(w, c_new))
            if not np.isfinite(score_new):
                continue

            if score_new < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = score_new
                best_p = pt.copy()
                p = pt
                cur_score = score_new
                prev_p = old_p
                accepted = True
                since_best = 0
                since_accept = 0
                cons_surr = 0
                break

            if score_new < cur_score - 1e-12:
                p = pt
                cur_score = score_new
                prev_p = old_p
                accepted = True
                since_accept = 0
                since_best += 1
                cons_surr = 0
                break

            if F_new < F - tol and score_new < cur_score + 0.004:
                p = pt
                cur_score = score_new
                prev_p = old_p
                accepted = True
                since_accept = 0
                since_best += 1
                cons_surr += 1
                if cons_surr > 40:
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    cur_score = best_score
                    prev_p = best_p.copy()
                    cons_surr = 0
                break

        if accepted:
            momentum = min(0.35, momentum + 0.01)
        else:
            since_accept += 1
            since_best += 1
            t_factor = max(0.004, t_factor * 0.6)
            topK = max(1, topK // 2)

            for sig in (0.08 / n, 0.02 / n, 0.005 / n, 0.001 / n):
                pt = project_simplex(p + rng.normal(0.0, float(sig), n))
                sc = score_normalized(pt)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and (sc < cur_score - 1e-12 or sc < best_score):
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_p = pt.copy()
                        since_best = 0
                    p = pt
                    cur_score = sc
                    prev_p = old_p
                    accepted = True
                    since_accept = 0
                    break

            if not accepted and n > 1:
                hi_idx = int(np.argmax(g))
                lo_idx = int(np.argmin(g))
                if hi_idx != lo_idx and g[hi_idx] > g[lo_idx] + 1e-300 and p[hi_idx] > 0.0:
                    for frac in (0.18, 0.06, 0.02, 0.005):
                        delta = float(frac) * float(p[hi_idx])
                        if delta <= 0.0:
                            continue
                        pt = p.copy()
                        pt[hi_idx] -= delta
                        pt[lo_idx] += delta
                        pt = project_simplex(pt)
                        sc = score_normalized(pt)
                        if np.isfinite(sc) and (sc < cur_score - 1e-12 or sc < best_score):
                            if sc < best_score:
                                best_score = sc
                                best_p = pt.copy()
                                since_best = 0
                            p = pt
                            cur_score = sc
                            prev_p = old_p
                            accepted = True
                            since_accept = 0
                            break

            if not accepted:
                since_best += 1

        if since_best > 800 or since_accept > 300:
            mix = 0.85 if best_score < 1.55 else 0.6
            p = project_simplex(
                mix * best_p + (1.0 - mix) * p + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05 / max(1, n), n)
            )
            cur_score = score_normalized(p)
            prev_p = p.copy()
            since_best = 0
            since_accept = 0
            cons_surr = 0
            momentum = 0.0
            t_factor = 0.20

        if it % 3000 == 0:
            print(label + " it=%d best=%.10f cur=%.10f" % (it, best_score, cur_score), flush=True)

        if best_score < 1.4980 and since_best > 5000:
            break

    best_p = project_simplex(best_p)
    best_score = score_normalized(best_p)
    return best_p, best_score


def lp_best_response(p, best_score, budget_s):
    if not HAVE_LP:
        return p, best_score
    p = safe_normalize(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64))
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 4 or n > LP_NMAX:
        return p, best_score
    end = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if end - time.monotonic() < 3.0:
        return p, best_score

    c = conv_sq(p)
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return p, best_score

    try:
        j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
        rows = (i[None, :] + j[:, None]).ravel()
        cols = np.repeat(j, n)
        data = np.tile(p, n).astype(np.float64)
        A = csr_matrix((data, (rows, cols)), shape=(2 * n - 1, n))
        obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for Mv in (M, 0.95 * M, 0.90 * M):
            if time.monotonic() > end - 5.0:
                break
            b = np.full(2 * n - 1, float(Mv), dtype=np.float64)
            try:
                res = linprog(
                    obj,
                    A_ub=A,
                    b_ub=b,
                    bounds=(0.0, None),
                    method="highs",
                    options={"disp": False}
                )
            except Exception:
                continue
            g = getattr(res, "x", None)
            if g is None:
                continue
            g = np.asarray(g, dtype=np.float64)
            g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
            g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
            s = float(g.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
                continue
            g = g / s
            for t in (1.0, 0.8, 0.55, 0.30, 0.10):
                q = project_simplex((1.0 - float(t)) * p + float(t) * g)
                sc = score_normalized(q)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                    best_score = float(sc)
                    p = q.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    return p, best_score


def add_candidate(cands, p, sc, limit=8):
    if p is None:
        return
    p = safe_normalize(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64))
    if p.size == 0:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = score_from_p(p)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    cands.append((float(sc), p.copy()))
    if len(cands) > limit:
        cands.sort(key=cand_key)
        del cands[limit - 1:]


def record_candidate(cands, best_p, best_score, p, sc):
    if p is None:
        return best_p, best_score
    p = safe_normalize(np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64))
    if p.size == 0:
        return best_p, best_score
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = score_from_p(p)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return best_p, best_score
    if sc < best_score:
        best_score = float(sc)
        best_p = p.copy()
    add_candidate(cands, p, best_score)
    return best_p, best_score


def evaluate_candidate_safe(p):
    try:
        seq = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)]
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            return float(val)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return np.inf


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2380):
    start = time.monotonic()
    try:
        b = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        b = 2380.0
    if not np.isfinite(b) or b <= 0.0:
        b = 2380.0
    b = min(b, 2390.0)
    deadline = start + b
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) if np.isfinite(seed) else 42)

    parent = None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
        arr = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.ndim == 1 and arr.size >= 32:
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            if float(arr.sum()) > 0.0:
                parent = safe_normalize(arr)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if deadline - start > 1500.0:
        final_n = 2048
    elif deadline - start > 600.0:
        final_n = 1536
    else:
        final_n = 1024

    cands = []
    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf

    if parent is not None:
        best_p, best_score = record_candidate(cands, best_p, best_score, parent, None)
        pr = resample_sequence(parent, final_n)
        best_p, best_score = record_candidate(cands, best_p, best_score, pr, None)
    else:
        u = np.full(final_n, 1.0 / final_n, dtype=np.float64)
        best_p, best_score = record_candidate(cands, best_p, best_score, u, None)

    init_p_list = []
    if parent is not None:
        init_p_list.append(parent)
        init_p_list.append(resample_sequence(parent, final_n))
    if best_p is not None:
        init_p_list.append(best_p)

    if deadline - time.monotonic() > 300.0:
        q_budget = min(850.0, max(120.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.35))
        try:
            q_p, q_s, _ = run_q_search(
                final_n,
                18,
                rng,
                q_budget,
                init_p_list,
                label="Q"
            )
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(cands, best_p, best_score, q_p, q_s)
            print("q search best=%.10f" % best_score, flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # If time remains, run a short-space/q search around the parent length.
    short_n = 0
    if parent is not None and 256 <= int(parent.size) <= 1000:
        short_n = int(parent.size)
    if short_n > 0 and deadline - time.monotonic() > 420.0:
        try:
            init_short = [parent]
            if best_p is not None:
                init_short.append(resample_sequence(best_p, short_n))
            q_budget2 = min(360.0, (deadline - time.monotonic()) * 0.28)
            q_p2, q_s2, _ = run_q_search(
                short_n,
                16,
                rng,
                q_budget2,
                init_short,
                label="Qs"
            )
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(cands, best_p, best_score, q_p2, q_s2)
            print("short q search best=%.10f" % best_score, flush=True)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Local refinement jobs.
    jobs = []
    if best_p is not None:
        jobs.append(("MAIN-FINAL", best_p))
    if parent is not None:
        jobs.append(("MAIN-PARENT", parent))
    if short_n > 0 and best_p is not None and int(best_p.size) != short_n:
        jobs.append(("LIFT-SHORT", resample_sequence(best_p, short_n)))

    # Deduplicate jobs by length and score.
    clean_jobs = []
    seen = set()
    for name, p in jobs:
        p = safe_normalize(p)
        key = (int(p.size), round(score_normalized(p), 12))
        if key not in seen:
            seen.add(key)
            clean_jobs.append((name, p))
    jobs = clean_jobs

    if jobs:
        while jobs and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 180.0:
            remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 120.0
            if remaining < 90.0:
                break
            name, p = jobs.pop(0)
            limit = min(600.0, max(90.0, remaining / max(1, len(jobs) + 1)))
            if name == "MAIN-FINAL":
                limit = min(1100.0, limit * 1.9)
            try:
                p2, s2 = local_refine(p, rng, limit, label=name + ":")
                best_p, best_score = record_candidate(cands, best_p, best_score, p2, s2)
                print("local %s done best=%.10f global=%.10f" % (name, s2, best_score), flush=True)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # LP best-response for a few moderate-length candidates.
    for p in list(cands):
        sc, pp = p
        if (deadline - time.monotonic()) < 60.0:
            break
        if 256 <= int(pp.size) <= LP_NMAX:
            try:
                lp_p, lp_s = lp_best_response(pp, best_score, min(90.0, max(20.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0)))
                best_p, best_score = record_candidate(cands, best_p, best_score, lp_p, lp_s)
            except Exception:
                pass

    # Final polish on the overall incumbent.
    if best_p is not None and deadline - time.monotonic() > 40.0:
        limit = max(10.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 18.0)
        try:
            p2, s2 = local_refine(best_p, rng, limit, label="FINAL:")
            best_p, best_score = record_candidate(cands, best_p, best_score, p2, s2)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Pick the best candidate by internal score, then by the hidden evaluator if available.
    chosen_p = best_p
    chosen_val = best_score

    cands.sort(key=cand_key)
    for sc, p in cands:
        if sc < chosen_val:
            chosen_val = sc
            chosen_p = p

    for sc, p in cands[:8]:
        try:
            ev = evaluate_candidate_safe(p)
            if np.isfinite(ev) and ev < chosen_val:
                chosen_val = ev
                chosen_p = p
        except Exception:
            pass

    if chosen_p is None or int(np.asarray(chosen_p).size) == 0:
        chosen_p = np.full(final_n, 1.0 / final_n, dtype=np.float64)

    chosen_p = safe_normalize(chosen_p)
    chosen_p = np.nan_to_num(chosen_p, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    chosen_p = np.maximum(chosen_p, 0.0)
    chosen_p = np.minimum(chosen_p, 1000.0)

    print("final internal best=%.10f n=%d" % (chosen_val, int(chosen_p.size)), flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in chosen_p]
```
